## 商定摄影背景架的组装顺序 · p10-a-01

主题：工作与协作；情景：确定摄影背景架的组装方案（photo-booth-frame-assembly-plan）。

沟通目标：确认摄影背景架的材料和部件，约定先组架再挂背景布

背景：摄影活动开始前，两位工作人员查看待组装的背景架及布料。

人物：Mina、Felix。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-01-s01 · Mina: Can you help me assemble the frame for the photo booth?
- p10-a-01-s02 · Felix: Sure. These poles are made of aluminum. Are they all the parts we need?
- p10-a-01-s03 · Mina: Yes, along with the feet in that box. The assembly instructions show where each pole goes.
- p10-a-01-s04 · Felix: And this canvas is the backdrop, right?
- p10-a-01-s05 · Mina: Right. Let's put the frame together while it's still bare, then hang the fabric.
- p10-a-01-s06 · Felix: Agreed. I'll unpack the feet, and we'll check the diagram before joining anything.

目标用法：

- assemble · v. · 组装；义项 sense-staged-ox-2fea91ab67518513-p10-a-01；来源 ox-2fea91ab67518513；证据 p10-a-01-s01；词形 assemble。
- aluminum · n. · 铝；义项 sense-staged-ox-cfbe65f41762f057-p10-a-01；来源 ox-cfbe65f41762f057；证据 p10-a-01-s02；词形 aluminum。
- assembly · n. · 组装；义项 sense-staged-ox-5b7d8cf820c2af0d-p10-a-01；来源 ox-5b7d8cf820c2af0d；证据 p10-a-01-s03；词形 assembly。
- canvas · n. · 帆布；义项 sense-staged-ox-bca5d6dcc0e3860e-p10-a-01；来源 ox-bca5d6dcc0e3860e；证据 p10-a-01-s04；词形 canvas。
- backdrop · n. · 摄影背景幕布；义项 sense-staged-ox-c3e6fa1308e9a336-p10-a-01；来源 ox-c3e6fa1308e9a336；证据 p10-a-01-s04；词形 backdrop。
- bare · adj. · 未覆盖的；义项 sense-staged-ox-0c7f645e6ddfca5a-p10-a-01；来源 ox-0c7f645e6ddfca5a；证据 p10-a-01-s05；词形 bare。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The photo-booth frame, aluminum poles, feet and canvas are explicitly identified in s01–s04. The speakers agree in s05–s06 to assemble the uncovered frame before hanging fabric; title, label and goal accurately describe an agreed sequence rather than completed construction.

## 排练业余音乐会的谢幕提示 · p10-a-02

主题：文娱与运动；情景：排练业余音乐会谢幕（amateur-concert-ending-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：明确贝司最后一音与谢幕动作的衔接，并试做鞠躬

背景：社区业余音乐会彩排时，一位乐手与舞台协调人核对结尾。

人物：Rosa、Dev。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-02-s01 · Rosa: This is my first amateur concert. What happens after I play the last note on the bass?
- p10-a-02-s02 · Dev: Let it fade, then look at me. I'll lower my hand when everyone is ready.
- p10-a-02-s03 · Rosa: Do we bow together, or does the singer go first?
- p10-a-02-s04 · Dev: Together. That gives the audience a clear moment to applaud.
- p10-a-02-s05 · Rosa: All right, I've finished the note. Your hand is down, so here goes.
- p10-a-02-s06 · Dev: That bow was well timed. Keep your eyes up until the cue, just as you did then.

目标用法：

- amateur · adj. · 业余的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ffe4808df1b3a21a-p10-a-02；来源 ox-ffe4808df1b3a21a；证据 p10-a-02-s01；词形 amateur。
- bass · n. · 贝司；低音乐器；义项 sense-staged-ox-dd3ea5100e7863ad-p10-a-02；来源 ox-dd3ea5100e7863ad；证据 p10-a-02-s01；词形 bass。
- bow · v. · 鞠躬；义项 sense-staged-ox-5d519995a72f0833-p10-a-02；来源 ox-5d519995a72f0833；证据 p10-a-02-s03；词形 bow。
- applaud · v. · 鼓掌；义项 sense-staged-ox-ee5d53324b242e21-p10-a-02；来源 ox-ee5d53324b242e21；证据 p10-a-02-s04；词形 applaud。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The concert is explicitly amateur, the instrument is bass, and s02–s04 specify the final-note/hand-signal/bow sequence. Crucially s05–s06 enact and assess a practice bow, supporting the completed rehearsal claim without implying the public concert has occurred.

绑定文件 SHA-256：6a67a8299120f72830bb4effe74a1570f7cd29f463707930031e8ab6a5f1dcef

- ox-ffe4808df1b3a21a：accepted；s01 places the adjective before concert, naturally describing a performance by nonprofessional musicians, consistent with the community rehearsal context.（p10-a-02-s01）。
- ox-dd3ea5100e7863ad：accepted；s01 uses the instrument noun after on the in play the last note on the bass; the selected 贝司 sense is clear, not a low voice or fish.（p10-a-02-s01）。
- ox-5d519995a72f0833：accepted；s03 uses the base verb after auxiliary do with we as subject; bow together asks about the performers’ physical gesture of 鞠躬.（p10-a-02-s03）。
- ox-ee5d53324b242e21：accepted；s04 uses the intransitive base verb in audience ... to applaud; this is clapping after the coordinated bow, matching 鼓掌.（p10-a-02-s04）。

## 商定大使到访时的欢迎致辞 · p10-a-03

主题：工作与协作；情景：限定大使欢迎致辞的代表范围（ambassador-welcome-speech-scope）。

沟通目标：明确致辞代表志愿者，并选定感谢大使到访的简短开场

背景：文化中心活动前，志愿者代表与协调人核对欢迎辞。

人物：Sana、Owen。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-03-s01 · Sana: The ambassador arrives at six. Would you like me to welcome her before the music starts?
- p10-a-03-s02 · Owen: Yes, but speak on behalf of our volunteers, not the whole city.
- p10-a-03-s03 · Sana: How about: 'Our volunteers would like to express our appreciation for your visit'?
- p10-a-03-s04 · Owen: That works. Follow it with a welcome to the centre, and then introduce the musicians.
- p10-a-03-s05 · Sana: I'll use that opening and keep the whole welcome under a minute.
- p10-a-03-s06 · Owen: Good. That's the version we'll put on your cue card.

目标用法：

- ambassador · n. · 大使；义项 sense-staged-ox-74e5046d03bdf36a-p10-a-03；来源 ox-74e5046d03bdf36a；证据 p10-a-03-s01；词形 ambassador。
- behalf · n. · 代表某人或群体（on behalf of）；义项 sense-staged-ox-039407bb11a06e45-p10-a-03；来源 ox-039407bb11a06e45；证据 p10-a-03-s02；词形 behalf。
- appreciation · n. · 感谢；义项 sense-staged-ox-37fa3f87632c3059-p10-a-03；来源 ox-37fa3f87632c3059；证据 p10-a-03-s03；词形 appreciation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The ambassador is explicitly introduced in s01. The restricted volunteer-representation scope in s02 is reflected in the proposed thanks in s03, accepted in s04 and confirmed in s05–s06. Metadata correctly describes selection of a short welcome, not its delivery.

- ox-039407bb11a06e45：accepted；s02 uses the noun within the fixed phrase on behalf of our volunteers; the contrast with not the whole city makes representation, not personal benefit, explicit.（p10-a-03-s02）。

## 约定社团章程修订的投票安排 · p10-a-04

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：安排社团章程修改表决（club-charter-amendment-ballot-plan）。

沟通目标：明确现行委员任命规则与修改提案，并约定下次会议投票前发送完整修订文字

背景：虚构摄影社团的秘书和主席讨论如何让会员决定任命规则。

人物：Ada、Jonas。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-04-s01 · Ada: Our charter says the chair can appoint the equipment committee without consulting members.
- p10-a-04-s02 · Jonas: I know. The last selection felt arbitrary because nobody explained the criteria.
- p10-a-04-s03 · Ada: I'd like to amend that section so members elect the committee instead.
- p10-a-04-s04 · Jonas: Then we need the full wording of the amendment before anyone votes.
- p10-a-04-s05 · Ada: I'll send it with next week's meeting notice. Can we hold a secret ballot at that meeting?
- p10-a-04-s06 · Jonas: Yes. Put the proposal on the agenda, but make clear that the current rule still applies until members approve a change.
- p10-a-04-s07 · Ada: Understood. I'll describe it as a proposal, not an adopted rule.

目标用法：

- charter · n. · 章程；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c6d71d6203900bb-p10-a-04；来源 ox-1c6d71d6203900bb；证据 p10-a-04-s01；词形 charter。
- appoint · v. · 任命；义项 sense-staged-ox-cc7da0165edb7bea-p10-a-04；来源 ox-cc7da0165edb7bea；证据 p10-a-04-s01；词形 appoint。
- arbitrary · adj. · 随意的；缺乏明确依据的；义项 sense-staged-ox-2b21f079507ff82a-p10-a-04；来源 ox-2b21f079507ff82a；证据 p10-a-04-s02；词形 arbitrary。
- amend · v. · 修订；义项 sense-staged-ox-578d63f2e5adb198-p10-a-04；来源 ox-578d63f2e5adb198；证据 p10-a-04-s03；词形 amend。
- amendment · n. · 修订条文；义项 sense-staged-ox-9be29427bd993c14-p10-a-04；来源 ox-9be29427bd993c14；证据 p10-a-04-s04；词形 amendment。
- ballot · n. · 投票；义项 sense-staged-ox-fc515beb75359f4a-p10-a-04；来源 ox-fc515beb75359f4a；证据 p10-a-04-s05；词形 ballot。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The club charter, appointment rule and election proposal are distinct throughout. S04–s06 agree to distribute full wording before a secret ballot at the next meeting, and s06–s07 explicitly preserve the current rule until approval. The title/label/goal and outcome evidence do not mistake a proposal for an adopted amendment.

## 用排队类比重新说明任务处理 · p10-a-05

主题：学习与技能；情景：练习用类比说明排队机制（presentation-analogy-clarity-practice）。

沟通目标：把含糊的技术说明改为排队类比，并确认听者理解其局限

背景：学生练习面向非技术听众介绍电脑的任务队列。

人物：Theo、Mei。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-05-s01 · Theo: I know what the program does, but I can't articulate it without using technical terms.
- p10-a-05-s02 · Mei: Try an analogy. Could you compare the waiting tasks to people in a queue?
- p10-a-05-s03 · Theo: All right. Each task takes a number and waits until a worker is free to handle it.
- p10-a-05-s04 · Mei: That helps with clarity. Does a big task always take the same time as a small one?
- p10-a-05-s05 · Theo: No. The queue explains the order, not how long each task takes.
- p10-a-05-s06 · Mei: Now I understand. Keep the queue example and that final warning in your presentation.

目标用法：

- articulate · v. · 清楚表达；义项 sense-staged-ox-38038943bbcfb635-p10-a-05；来源 ox-38038943bbcfb635；证据 p10-a-05-s01；词形 articulate。
- analogy · n. · 类比；义项 sense-staged-ox-e86f1f7796ab6569-p10-a-05；来源 ox-e86f1f7796ab6569；证据 p10-a-05-s02；词形 analogy。
- clarity · n. · 清晰；易懂；义项 sense-staged-ox-9316b1c38d0377e8-p10-a-05；来源 ox-9316b1c38d0377e8；证据 p10-a-05-s04；词形 clarity。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The student’s difficulty explaining without jargon motivates an actual queue analogy in s03; s05 limits it to processing order rather than duration. S06 explicitly confirms comprehension. Title and label fit the presentation practice, and the goal’s clarity problem is resolved in speech rather than merely promised.

- ox-38038943bbcfb635：accepted；s01 uses the transitive base verb after cannot, with it referring to what the program does; the difficulty is expressing an explanation clearly, not physical articulation.（p10-a-05-s01）。
- ox-e86f1f7796ab6569：accepted；s02 uses the singular count noun after an and immediately proposes comparing waiting tasks with people in a queue; this concretely instantiates 类比.（p10-a-05-s02）。

## 选定海湾内停泊的游船路线 · p10-a-06

主题：出行与住宿；情景：确定海湾停泊的游船路线（sailing-anchor-bay-route-choice）。

沟通目标：确认短程游船的停泊地点，并选择留在海湾内的路线

背景：游客在出发前向游船船长询问两条当日可选路线。

人物：Imani、Captain Ellis。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-06-s01 · Imani: Does the short boat trip leave the bay?
- p10-a-06-s02 · Captain Ellis: No. We stay inside it and stop near the sheltered eastern shore.
- p10-a-06-s03 · Imani: Will we keep moving while we have lunch?
- p10-a-06-s04 · Captain Ellis: We'll drop the anchor at the lunch stop, if conditions remain suitable. I'll make that decision on the water.
- p10-a-06-s05 · Imani: Then I'll choose the short route. I don't want the trip out along the coast.
- p10-a-06-s06 · Captain Ellis: The bay route it is. Please join the safety briefing before we board.

目标用法：

- bay · n. · 海湾；义项 sense-staged-ox-c58567f40e93bcbb-p10-a-06；来源 ox-c58567f40e93bcbb；证据 p10-a-06-s01；词形 bay。
- anchor · n. · 锚；义项 sense-staged-ox-10194da2bd9d6834-p10-a-06；来源 ox-10194da2bd9d6834；证据 p10-a-06-s04；词形 anchor。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The route stays inside the bay and stops by the sheltered eastern shore (s01–s02); anchoring at lunch is expressly conditional on conditions (s04). S05–s06 select that route, not an already completed trip. The title, label and goal describe the chosen route and its planned stopping location and do not remove the captain’s safety discretion.

- ox-10194da2bd9d6834：accepted；s04 uses the physical count noun in drop the anchor at a boat’s lunch stop, the ordinary nautical collocation for lowering an anchor, not a verb or figurative support.（p10-a-06-s04）。

## 选定奇幻故事封面的双角色画面 · p10-a-07

主题：文娱与运动；情景：选定奇幻故事封面角色（fantasy-book-cover-character-choice）。

沟通目标：依据故事角色关系，选择天使与野兽友好相处的封面草图

人物：Lucia、Pavel。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-07-s01 · Lucia: The winged figure is an angel, and the enormous creature beside her is the beast from chapter one.
- p10-a-07-s02 · Pavel: Readers may expect a fight. Isn't the creature actually her beloved companion?
- p10-a-07-s03 · Lucia: Exactly. In the story she rescues him, and they become friends.
- p10-a-07-s04 · Pavel: Then the sketch where he's breathing fire at her gives a bizarre impression of their relationship.
- p10-a-07-s05 · Lucia: Let's use the other one, where she's sitting beside him and he's asleep.
- p10-a-07-s06 · Pavel: Agreed. I'll develop that quiet scene for the cover.

目标用法：

- angel · n. · 天使；义项 sense-staged-ox-e3f6e4b3a72068da-p10-a-07；来源 ox-e3f6e4b3a72068da；证据 p10-a-07-s01；词形 angel。

S05–s06 select the quiet paired sketch and promise further development. The title, label and goal correctly describe an agreed cover-image choice, not a finished illustration.

## 重写经济学作文中过强的断言 · p10-a-15

主题：学习与技能；情景：修改经济学作文的绝对化断言（economics-essay-claim-revision）。

沟通目标：区分资本主义制度与单个企业案例，并将普遍化断言改为限定于案例的评价

背景：虚构课堂作文反馈中，师生仅依据给定案例讨论论证范围。

人物：Miles、Dr Chen。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-15-s01 · Miles: My essay says capitalism always rewards the most useful businesses. Is that too strong?
- p10-a-15-s02 · Dr Chen: Yes. You can't characterize an entire economic system from one successful company.
- p10-a-15-s03 · Miles: But the case study calls its setting a capitalist economy.
- p10-a-15-s04 · Dr Chen: That describes the setting. It doesn't support your assertion about what always happens there.
- p10-a-15-s05 · Miles: Could I assert that this particular company served its customers well?
- p10-a-15-s06 · Dr Chen: The customer interviews support that, although the survey is small. Keep that limit visible.
- p10-a-15-s07 · Miles: I've changed it to: 'The company was arguably successful at meeting the needs of the customers interviewed.'
- p10-a-15-s08 · Dr Chen: That's a claim the case can support. Keep the next sentence about the small sample too.

目标用法：

- capitalism · n. · 资本主义；义项 sense-staged-ox-8bbcdd3c74cccd23-p10-a-15；来源 ox-8bbcdd3c74cccd23；证据 p10-a-15-s01；词形 capitalism。
- characterize · v. · 描述……的特征；义项 sense-staged-ox-7fefc09388f7e66c-p10-a-15；来源 ox-7fefc09388f7e66c；证据 p10-a-15-s02；词形 characterize。
- capitalist · adj. · 资本主义的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8a02ffb26dcfbba5-p10-a-15；来源 ox-8a02ffb26dcfbba5；证据 p10-a-15-s03；词形 capitalist。
- assertion · n. · 断言；主张；义项 sense-staged-ox-1b464571404dbdce-p10-a-15；来源 ox-1b464571404dbdce；证据 p10-a-15-s04；词形 assertion。
- assert · v. · 断言；明确主张；义项 sense-staged-ox-751e5e54428d50b3-p10-a-15；来源 ox-751e5e54428d50b3；证据 p10-a-15-s05；词形 assert。
- arguably · adv. · 可以认为；有理由认为；义项 sense-staged-ox-4303cef7abc40f22-p10-a-15；来源 ox-4303cef7abc40f22；证据 p10-a-15-s07；词形 arguably。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The classroom exercise criticizes an unsupported universal claim about capitalism and separates the economic setting from a single company’s evidence. S07 actually supplies a replacement limited to interviewed customers and s08 accepts it while preserving the small sample limit. Metadata correctly claims a completed revision, with no unsupported real-world economic conclusion.

- ox-7fefc09388f7e66c：accepted；s02 uses the transitive base verb after cannot with an entire economic system as object; it means describe the system’s nature from evidence, matching 描述……的特征.（p10-a-15-s02）。

- p10-a-16-s03 · Ruth: No. The barrel has no label, and I didn't see who brought it.
- p10-a-16-s04 · Ben: Then caution seems sensible. Let's leave it alone rather than add an unknown material.
- p10-a-16-s05 · Ruth: I'll ask the garden manager what it is and how it should be dealt with.
- p10-a-16-s06 · Ben: Good. We'll wait for that answer before moving any of it.

## 把技术职业愿望化成首个练习目标 · p10-a-17

沟通目标：说明长远职业愿望，并约定用一份可独立复现的入门项目作为首个学习检查点

- p10-a-17-s04 · Simon: Then let's use one small project as a benchmark: a page you can explain and rebuild on your own.
- p10-a-17-s05 · Yara: That sounds manageable. I'll make a one-page guide to our local walking group.
- p10-a-17-s06 · Simon: Bring it to our final tutorial. We'll look at what you can do independently and choose the next step from there.

The long-term aim is accessible web design and eventual professional work, but the near-term criterion is explaining and independently rebuilding a simple page. - p10-a-18-s05 · Priya: Let's not show a clip. We can discuss the review's argument without asking everyone to watch that.
- p10-a-18-s06 · Marcus: Agreed. I'll prepare a short written summary with a content note about violence, and people can choose whether to join that discussion.

## 写出区分保释与判决的课堂摘要 · p10-a-19

- p10-a-19-s02 · Mr Reed: No. Look at the next line: the trial hasn't taken place. Bail tells you about his release while the case continues, not the verdict.
- p10-a-19-s03 · Tessa: So I shouldn't write that he committed the attack either?
- p10-a-19-s04 · Mr Reed: Correct. Report the charge as a charge, and keep it separate from any finding of guilt.
- p10-a-19-s05 · Tessa: I've written: 'The man faces an assault charge and has been released on bail ahead of his trial.'
- p10-a-19-s06 · Mr Reed: That accurately reflects the information in this exercise.

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional classroom report explicitly distinguishes an assault charge, release on bail and a trial not yet held. S03–s04 rule out asserting guilt, and s05 actually gives a neutral written summary that the teacher accepts. - p10-a-22-s05 · Imogen: Please put those conditions in writing. We'll review them before deciding whether to accept.

## 调整篮球运球后将球留在界内 · p10-a-28

## 暂不把缓存改进称为整机突破 · p10-a-29

主题：研究与论证；情景：限定原型缓存测试的结论（prototype-buffer-test-claim-scope）。

沟通目标：区分原型缓存暂存更多读数的表现与整机长期可靠性，并约定补做对照及长时间测试

背景：设备研发同事核对原型的一次短时测试报告。

人物：Iris、Omar。

起草：author-phase10-a；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-a-29-s01 · Iris: The larger buffer held more readings before transmission. Can we call that a breakthrough?
- p10-a-29-s02 · Omar: Not yet. It shows a useful capability, but it was only a ten-minute run.
- p10-a-29-s03 · Iris: There was no breakdown during the run, though.
- p10-a-29-s04 · Omar: True, but that doesn't establish long-term reliability. We also changed the connection settings.
- p10-a-29-s05 · Iris: So we can't attribute the whole improvement to the buffer alone.
- p10-a-29-s06 · Omar: Exactly. Let's compare the two buffer sizes with the same settings, then run the better version for longer.
- p10-a-29-s07 · Iris: Agreed. I'll describe this as an initial result and add those tests to the plan.

- breakthrough · n. · 突破性进展；义项 sense-staged-ox-f886227b6487669f-p10-a-29；来源 ox-f886227b6487669f；证据 p10-a-29-s01；词形 breakthrough。

- attribute · v. · 把……归因于；义项 sense-staged-ox-06f5764a7708c07b-p10-a-29；来源 ox-06f5764a7708c07b；证据 p10-a-29-s05；词形 attribute。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-a；整篇结论：accepted。

Full changed-file rereview: s01 reports more readings held before transmission, s02 limits the run to ten minutes, and s03–s04 distinguish no failure in that run from long-term device reliability. Changed connection settings make sole attribution premature. The revised goal and outcome clause accurately name 原型缓存暂存更多读数的表现 rather than claiming improved transmission performance. - p10-a-37-s03 · Mira: Does it continue after I leave?
- p10-a-37-s04 · Daniel: Not usually. Yesterday she joined the painting table within a few minutes.
- p10-a-37-s05 · Mira: Could we record what happens at the goodbye and how she settles afterward for a week?
- p10-a-37-s06 · Daniel: Yes. I'll note those specific observations, and we'll review them together next Friday.
- p10-a-37-s07 · Mira: That would help. Please replace the broad label in today's note with what you actually observed.

- p10-b-05-s02 · Agent: I can see the incident report. Are you asking us to compensate you for the repair?
- p10-b-05-s03 · Nadia: Yes. I have a photograph and an estimate from the bicycle shop.
- p10-b-05-s04 · Agent: Please send both through this claim form. I can submit them for review, but I cannot confirm compensation or an amount today.
- p10-b-05-s05 · Nadia: All right. I'll attach them this afternoon. When should I ask for an update?
- p10-b-05-s06 · Agent: If you haven't heard from the claims team by Friday, reply to the confirmation email with your reference number.

## 确定港口贸易展板的材料范围 · p10-b-06

- p10-b-06-s03 · Aisha: Would that make the panel too narrow?
- p10-b-06-s04 · Jonah: Not if we use the copper trade to explain commerce through this port. The ledger gives us destinations and quantities.
- p10-b-06-s05 · Aisha: We also need to say who kept the ledger and that it comes from the period of colonial rule.
- p10-b-06-s06 · Jonah: Agreed. I'll build the panel around those copper entries and include the archive reference, without guessing who profited.

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The display task narrows a fictional port ledger to copper, destinations and quantities. s05-s06 add colonial context and an archive reference without inventing profits. Title/label describe the chosen scope, and goal/outcome evidence correctly separate that decision from future panel production.

- p10-b-07-s04 · Tutor: And where does the new constitution fit?
- p10-b-07-s05 · Leo: It was approved the following year. A separate note calls that a constitutional change, not another military takeover.
- p10-b-07-s06 · Tutor: Then give each event its own date. What will you do with the claim about an immediate change of government?
- p10-b-07-s07 · Leo: I'll remove that claim and use three separate entries, each linked to the page that supports it.

- p10-b-09-s05 · Tessa: I want the investigation to help combat misuse of public money, not spread a rumour.
- p10-b-09-s06 · Editor: Then start by checking the spreadsheet against the records. We'll hold the accusatory headline until we have evidence we can stand behind.

## 限定课堂调查的相关性表述 · p10-b-13

主题：研究与论证；情景：限定课堂调查的相关性表述（class-survey-correlation-wording）。

沟通目标：核对两组记录是否可比，并约定重新计算后只描述相关而不宣称因果

- p10-b-13-s01 · Mei: These scores seem to correlate with the hours of sleep people reported. Can I say sleep improved memory?
- p10-b-13-s02 · Hugo: Not from this class survey alone. Were the two groups given comparable tests?
- p10-b-13-s03 · Mei: Actually, one group had twenty pictures and the other had thirty. I used the raw number remembered.
- p10-b-13-s04 · Hugo: Then compute the proportion remembered before comparing them. Even that won't remove every difference between the tests.
- p10-b-13-s05 · Mei: Right. If the pattern remains, I'll describe a correlation in our sample, not a cause.
- p10-b-13-s06 · Hugo: And include the different test lengths as a limitation.
- p10-b-13-s07 · Mei: I will. I'll redo the calculation before writing the conclusion.

- correlation · n. · 相关关系；义项 sense-staged-ox-b4b366f990ab55fa-p10-b-13；来源 ox-b4b366f990ab55fa；证据 p10-b-13-s05；词形 correlation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The class-survey discussion discovers differing test lengths, proposes proportions while retaining limitations, and explicitly rejects causal inference. s05 makes reporting correlation conditional on the pattern remaining, and s07 promises recalculation. Title/label and goal describe a bounded reporting plan, not completed analysis; the outcome evidence supports each clause.

## 承认打破花瓶并约定归还碎片 · p10-b-14

- p10-b-14-s03 · Felix: I was embarrassed. I tried to conceal the damage by moving the pieces into a box, but my conscience wouldn't let me leave it there.
- p10-b-14-s04 · Alma: I can't compel you to be honest with me. I do need you to tell me when something of mine gets damaged.
- p10-b-14-s05 · Felix: You're right. I'm sorry. I kept all the pieces. Would you like me to bring the box back?
- p10-b-14-s06 · Alma: Yes. Please don't throw anything away or try to glue it. We'll look at it together.
- p10-b-14-s07 · Felix: I'll bring the box this evening and leave it as it is.

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

A coherent admission and relationship repair: Felix confesses both breakage and concealment, Alma asks for honesty and undisturbed pieces, and Felix agrees to return them that evening. 沟通目标：指出预算限制和报价缺项，并约定先索取逐项说明再决定是否施工

背景：虚构社区会堂项目：管理员与项目同事阅读通风改造报价，不提供工程或法规建议。

人物：Gareth、Inez。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-18-s01 · Inez: The contractor has sent a single price for the hall ventilation work. It is more costly than we expected.
- p10-b-18-s02 · Gareth: Does it say what is included?
- p10-b-18-s03 · Inez: Not clearly. The note mentions the complexity of working above the stage, but there is no breakdown.
- p10-b-18-s04 · Gareth: Our budget is a real constraint. We also need a clear statement about which requirements the design must comply with.
- p10-b-18-s05 · Inez: There is a line saying 'compliance included', but no named standard or inspection step.
- p10-b-18-s06 · Gareth: Ask for an itemised scope and the standards they are using. We cannot judge the quote from that phrase alone.
- p10-b-18-s07 · Inez: I'll request those details and a separate price for any optional work. We won't authorise construction until we can compare the complete figures.

目标用法：

- contractor · n. · 承包商；义项 sense-staged-ox-00d40e77ad8cbcb0-p10-b-18；来源 ox-00d40e77ad8cbcb0；证据 p10-b-18-s01；词形 contractor。
- costly · adj. · 昂贵的；义项 sense-staged-ox-68a9b9bc52f867b8-p10-b-18；来源 ox-68a9b9bc52f867b8；证据 p10-b-18-s01；词形 costly。
- complexity · n. · 复杂性；义项 sense-staged-ox-671176ceeb044596-p10-b-18；来源 ox-671176ceeb044596；证据 p10-b-18-s03；词形 complexity。
- constraint · n. · 限制因素；义项 sense-staged-ox-fc00f7d730f17052-p10-b-18；来源 ox-fc00f7d730f17052；证据 p10-b-18-s04；词形 constraint。
- comply · v. · 遵守；符合；义项 sense-staged-ox-42b9e2a25eab1469-p10-b-18；来源 ox-42b9e2a25eab1469；证据 p10-b-18-s04；词形 comply。
- compliance · n. · 合规；符合要求；义项 sense-staged-ox-5d2a551222434567-p10-b-18；来源 ox-5d2a551222434567；证据 p10-b-18-s05；词形 compliance。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The hall ventilation scope, unexpectedly high price, stage-work complexity and absent breakdown form a coherent quote-review task. The only compliance statement lacks named standards; s06-s07 agree to request itemised scope and optional-work pricing before authorization. Title/label and goal do not falsely claim receipt, compliance certification or construction approval; outcome evidence matches.

## 列出医疗操作咨询时要问的问题 · p10-b-19

主题：健康与照护；情景：列出医疗操作咨询时要问的问题（procedure-consultation-question-list）。

沟通目标：说明对意识状态和潜在并发症的疑问，并约定带书面问题向临床人员核实

背景：虚构就医准备：成年人请朋友帮忙整理将向医生提出的问题，不提供诊疗判断。

人物：Paula、Omar。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-19-s01 · Omar: My consultation about the procedure is tomorrow, and I keep forgetting what I want to ask.
- p10-b-19-s02 · Paula: What is your main concern?
- p10-b-19-s03 · Omar: Whether I'll lose consciousness at any point. The leaflet names several options, but I don't know which one they mean for me.
- p10-b-19-s04 · Paula: Write that down first. What else?
- p10-b-19-s05 · Omar: It mentions a possible complication without explaining how likely it is in my situation.
- p10-b-19-s06 · Paula: Then ask the clinician to explain that risk and the alternatives. I cannot tell from the leaflet either.
- p10-b-19-s07 · Omar: I've written both questions here. I'll take this list and ask before making a decision.

目标用法：

- consultation · n. · 专业咨询；诊疗咨询；义项 sense-staged-ox-3db93d41699f4386-p10-b-19；来源 ox-3db93d41699f4386；证据 p10-b-19-s01；词形 consultation。
- consciousness · n. · 清醒意识状态；义项 sense-staged-ox-8a984e5d182f9612-p10-b-19；来源 ox-8a984e5d182f9612；证据 p10-b-19-s03；词形 consciousness。
- complication · n. · 并发症；义项 sense-staged-ox-0069444adf6bda40-p10-b-19；来源 ox-0069444adf6bda40；证据 p10-b-19-s05；词形 complication。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The consultation-preparation dialogue retains a generic medical procedure rather than inferring surgery. s03/s05 identify consciousness and complication questions; s07 explicitly confirms both written down and promises to take them to the clinician before deciding. Title/label, goal and outcome evidence distinguish the completed question list from unanswered clinical questions.

- ox-8a984e5d182f9612：accepted；s03 lose consciousness is the standard noun collocation for losing the waking/aware state, not moral conscience or public awareness.（p10-b-19-s03）。

## 就社区花园关门时间达成试行折中 · p10-b-20

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：就社区花园关门时间达成试行折中（garden-gate-hours-compromise）。

沟通目标：说明分歧并商定本月工作日七点、周末八点关门的试行方案，同时保留月底复核

背景：社区花园两位轮值管理员得到各自小组授权，商议本月试行的关门时间。

人物：Helen、Ravi。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-20-s01 · Ravi: The point of contention is still the closing time. Your group wants eight every night; ours wants six.
- p10-b-20-s02 · Helen: Could a compromise be seven on weekdays and eight at weekends?
- p10-b-20-s03 · Ravi: I concede that six is too early for people coming from work, but our weekday volunteer leaves at seven.
- p10-b-20-s04 · Helen: Seven on weekdays is a concession we can accept. Could your group cover eight at weekends?
- p10-b-20-s05 · Ravi: We can cover that for this month. We would need to review the workload afterwards.
- p10-b-20-s06 · Helen: Then do we have a consensus on seven during the week and eight at weekends, with a review at the end of the month?
- p10-b-20-s07 · Ravi: Yes. I'll tell our rota organiser those trial hours and the review date.

目标用法：

- contention · n. · 争论；争执；义项 sense-staged-ox-8d9f060666c2cb66-p10-b-20；来源 ox-8d9f060666c2cb66；证据 p10-b-20-s01；词形 contention。
- compromise · n. · 折中方案；义项 sense-staged-ox-205bd05a61243ca8-p10-b-20；来源 ox-205bd05a61243ca8；证据 p10-b-20-s02；词形 compromise。
- concede · v. · 承认原先未接受的观点；义项 sense-staged-ox-0fb743f4957b1225-p10-b-20；来源 ox-0fb743f4957b1225；证据 p10-b-20-s03；词形 concede。
- concession · n. · 让步；义项 sense-staged-ox-3a13028e029af38a-p10-b-20；来源 ox-3a13028e029af38a；证据 p10-b-20-s04；词形 concession。
- consensus · n. · 共识；义项 sense-staged-ox-d1fb005ab3a56cdd-p10-b-20；来源 ox-d1fb005ab3a56cdd；证据 p10-b-20-s06；词形 consensus。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The stated context grants the garden representatives group authority. s01 contrasts six and eight, s03-s05 establishes staffing limits and weekend coverage, and s06-s07 explicitly accepts seven on weekdays/eight at weekends for this month with end-month review. Title/label and each goal clause accurately describe a completed trial-hours agreement plus future review, not a permanent policy.

- ox-205bd05a61243ca8：accepted；s02 a compromise is a count noun for the proposed middle-ground schedule combining earlier weekday and later weekend closure.（p10-b-20-s02）。
- ox-0fb743f4957b1225：accepted；s03 I concede that six is too early uses the verb with a that-clause to acknowledge a previously resisted point, matching the chosen Chinese sense.（p10-b-20-s03）。

## 约定先画出轮椅可参与的游乐区草图 · p10-b-21

主题：工作与协作；情景：约定先画出轮椅可参与的游乐区草图（accessible-playground-first-sketch）。

沟通目标：澄清游乐区的初步构想，并约定把一起玩的需求画入草图后再向家庭征求意见

背景：社区设计工作坊，两位成员准备一个尚未通过的游乐区概念方案。

人物：Mabel、Arun。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-21-s01 · Arun: Can we conceive of a playground where children using wheelchairs can join the same games rather than stay in a separate area?
- p10-b-21-s02 · Mabel: Yes. My initial conception was a central play table with space to approach from different sides.
- p10-b-21-s03 · Arun: That could work, but the table alone would not meet every need. We should ask families what activities they want to share.
- p10-b-21-s04 · Mabel: Would a rough drawing help them respond, or would it look as though we had already decided?
- p10-b-21-s05 · Arun: Let's label it as an idea for discussion. I'll sketch several approach spaces and leave room for their suggestions.
- p10-b-21-s06 · Mabel: Good. We'll take that draft to the next workshop, not present it as a finished design.

目标用法：

- conceive · v. · 设想；构想；义项 sense-staged-ox-d698aa5caf172bb2-p10-b-21；来源 ox-d698aa5caf172bb2；证据 p10-b-21-s01；词形 conceive。
- conception · n. · 初步构想；义项 sense-staged-ox-545a85f8a400b24c-p10-b-21；来源 ox-545a85f8a400b24c；证据 p10-b-21-s02；词形 conception。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The design-workshop context and s01 establish wheelchair-using children and inclusive shared games. s02-s03 clarify a central table as an incomplete initial concept, and s05-s06 promise a labelled discussion sketch for family feedback. Title/label and goal correctly describe the plan rather than a finished or proven-accessible design; outcome evidence keeps drawing and consultation future.

- ox-545a85f8a400b24c：accepted；s02 My initial conception was a central play table uses the noun for the initial design idea, concretely developed and qualified in subsequent turns.（p10-b-21-s02）。

## 约定停止贬低朋友的工作 · p10-b-22

主题：家庭与人际；情景：约定停止贬低朋友的工作（friends-comment-boundary-discussion）。

沟通目标：说明贬低言语造成的感受，并在不扩大争执的情况下确认以后不再这样评论

背景：两位朋友私下谈论聚会时一人嘲笑另一人工作的事情。

人物：Seth、Imani。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-22-s01 · Imani: I wanted to confront you about what you said at dinner. Calling my work pointless was hurtful.
- p10-b-22-s02 · Seth: I was joking. You don't have to condemn everything I say.
- p10-b-22-s03 · Imani: I'm talking about that remark, not everything. It sounded as though you felt contempt for work that matters to me.
- p10-b-22-s04 · Seth: I'm getting defensive. Let me start again. I didn't think about how it would sound.
- p10-b-22-s05 · Imani: I don't want a confrontation every time we meet. I do want you to stop making my job the joke.
- p10-b-22-s06 · Seth: You're right. I'm sorry for that comment. I won't make those jokes again.
- p10-b-22-s07 · Imani: Thank you. That's the change I was asking for.

目标用法：

- confront · v. · 当面提出问题或质问；义项 sense-staged-ox-9b3e4d22fa016ca0-p10-b-22；来源 ox-9b3e4d22fa016ca0；证据 p10-b-22-s01；词形 confront。
- condemn · v. · 强烈谴责；批评；义项 sense-staged-ox-569ab46d5139d92e-p10-b-22；来源 ox-569ab46d5139d92e；证据 p10-b-22-s02；词形 condemn。
- contempt · n. · 轻蔑；义项 sense-staged-ox-4da4515c19af2a8a-p10-b-22；来源 ox-4da4515c19af2a8a；证据 p10-b-22-s03；词形 contempt。
- defensive · adj. · 带有自我辩护态度的；义项 sense-staged-ox-544b8a950e730e8b-p10-b-22；来源 ox-544b8a950e730e8b；证据 p10-b-22-s04；词形 defensive。
- confrontation · n. · 冲突；对抗；义项 sense-staged-ox-cfaf42f730556c46-p10-b-22；来源 ox-cfaf42f730556c46；证据 p10-b-22-s05；词形 confrontation。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The private friends conversation addresses a specific hurtful job joke. Initial defensiveness is repaired, Imani limits the complaint to that remark, and Seth explicitly apologizes and promises no recurrence in s06, accepted in s07. Title/label and goal represent a boundary agreement, not proof of future behaviour; evidence accurately separates expressed feelings from promised change.

- ox-9b3e4d22fa016ca0：accepted；s01 confront you about what you said uses a transitive verb for directly challenging a person about conduct; the subsequent specific complaint makes 当面提出问题或质问 clear.（p10-b-22-s01）。
- ox-569ab46d5139d92e：accepted；s02 condemn everything I say uses the transitive verb for strongly criticizing remarks; Seth defensive exaggeration is corrected in s03 and is not the narrator claim.（p10-b-22-s02）。
- ox-4da4515c19af2a8a：accepted；s03 felt contempt for work uses the abstract noun in the natural contempt for construction for dismissive disdain, framed as perceived tone rather than proven motive.（p10-b-22-s03）。
- ox-544b8a950e730e8b：accepted；s04 I am getting defensive uses a predicative adjective for self-protective resistance to criticism, explicitly exemplified by s02.（p10-b-22-s04）。

## 依社团规则暂缓人数不足的表决 · p10-b-23

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：依社团规则暂缓人数不足的表决（club-quorum-decision-rule）。

沟通目标：核对出席人数与法定人数，并决定先交换意见而等第四名成员到场再表决

背景：虚构摄影社委员会开会，秘书拿出社团现行规则核对表决程序。

人物：Secretary、Chair。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-23-s01 · Chair: Only three committee members are here. Can we approve the equipment purchase now?
- p10-b-23-s02 · Secretary: Our rules say four members constitute a quorum for spending decisions.
- p10-b-23-s03 · Chair: Could we confer about the options while we wait, without taking a vote?
- p10-b-23-s04 · Secretary: Yes. Discussion is allowed, but the decision-making rule still applies to approval.
- p10-b-23-s05 · Chair: Then we will compare the two quotes now and wait for the fourth member before voting.
- p10-b-23-s06 · Secretary: I'll record that no purchase has been approved at this point.

目标用法：

- constitute · v. · 构成；义项 sense-staged-ox-a4569f1bf0004a3f-p10-b-23；来源 ox-a4569f1bf0004a3f；证据 p10-b-23-s02；词形 constitute。
- confer · v. · 商议；义项 sense-staged-ox-9e8be0959c158a72-p10-b-23；来源 ox-9e8be0959c158a72；证据 p10-b-23-s03；词形 confer。
- decision-making · n. · 决策过程；义项 sense-staged-ox-fd162b36ed5a3616-p10-b-23；来源 ox-fd162b36ed5a3616；证据 p10-b-23-s04；词形 decision-making。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional club rule requires four members while only three attend. Discussion and quote comparison are allowed, but s05-s06 defer voting and explicitly record no purchase approval. Title/label and goal accurately describe a procedural pause and future vote contingent on attendance; the outcome evidence does not claim procurement authorization.

- ox-9e8be0959c158a72：accepted；s03 confer about the options uses the intransitive discussion verb with about; this is 商议 rather than confer an honour.（p10-b-23-s03）。

## 安排教会志愿者的简短致谢 · p10-b-24

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：安排教会志愿者的简短致谢（church-volunteer-thank-you-plan）。

沟通目标：选定感谢志愿者的具体工作，并尊重本人不愿上台讲话的意愿

背景：教会活动负责人询问长期志愿者，希望在周末聚会表达感谢。

人物：Noor、Peter。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-24-s01 · Peter: The congregation would like to thank you for keeping the weekly meal service going.
- p10-b-24-s02 · Noor: That's kind, but please don't ask me to give a speech.
- p10-b-24-s03 · Peter: You have been a dedicated volunteer for years. Would a short thank-you from me be comfortable?
- p10-b-24-s04 · Noor: Yes, if you mention the whole kitchen team. They do much more than people see.
- p10-b-24-s05 · Peter: I will. I'll thank the team for their dedication and mention the early starts and the washing-up, not just the cooking.
- p10-b-24-s06 · Noor: That sounds lovely. A few words while I stay in my seat would be perfect.

- congregation · n. · 教会会众；义项 sense-staged-ox-4d0ecedcab0b367e-p10-b-24；来源 ox-4d0ecedcab0b367e；证据 p10-b-24-s01；词形 congregation。

- dedication · n. · 奉献精神；义项 sense-staged-ox-0c1693bbd60e4ac2-p10-b-24；来源 ox-0c1693bbd60e4ac2；证据 p10-b-24-s05；词形 dedication。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

Church identity is explicit in the context and congregation in s01. The planned thanks covers meal-service teamwork, early starts and washing-up, while Noor requests no speech and accepts remaining seated. Title/label and both goal clauses correctly describe an agreed future acknowledgement with concrete content, not an event already held.

主题：自然与环境；情景：选择连续两天的清晨观鸟记录班次（bird-survey-morning-slot）。

沟通目标：核对观鸟记录所需日期和集合时间，并约好领取记录表

- p10-b-25-s02 · Coordinator: The team needs the same observer on Monday and Tuesday for this comparison. We meet at the park gate at dawn.
- p10-b-25-s03 · Jules: What time does that mean on the schedule?
- p10-b-25-s04 · Coordinator: The meeting time is six fifteen on both days. The gate will be open for our group.
- p10-b-25-s05 · Jules: I can do those two mornings. Where do I get the recording sheet?
- p10-b-25-s06 · Coordinator: I'll bring it to Sunday's briefing. Come to that, and we'll go through the route before the first count.
- p10-b-25-s07 · Jules: Sunday works for me. I'll collect the sheet at the briefing and go over the route with you.

New s07 explicitly accepts Sunday briefing and collection of the recording sheet, answering the coordinator promise in s06; the previously unsupported mutual pickup agreement is now established. Title/label, both goal clauses, context and revised outcome evidence accurately distinguish chosen shifts and agreed collection from future observation, pickup and route review.

## 决定先了解校园咨询服务 · p10-b-28

主题：健康与照护；情景：决定先了解校园咨询服务（us-campus-counseling-inquiry）。

沟通目标：表达对公开发言的顾虑，并选择先阅读首次会谈说明而不立即预约

背景：虚构美国大学：学生和同学讨论如何了解校园心理咨询服务，不作诊断或治疗建议。

人物：Dylan、Maya。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-28-s01 · Maya: Every presentation makes me want to skip class. I'm starting to contemplate dropping the course.
- p10-b-28-s02 · Dylan: Would you consider talking to a counselor before making that decision?
- p10-b-28-s03 · Maya: Maybe. I keep telling myself I should conquer my nerves alone.
- p10-b-28-s04 · Dylan: You don't have to prove anything by doing it alone. The campus counseling service has a page explaining how the first conversation works.
- p10-b-28-s05 · Maya: Could we look at that page together? I want to know what I would be agreeing to.
- p10-b-28-s06 · Dylan: Sure. We can read it now, and you can decide whether to contact them.
- p10-b-28-s07 · Maya: Let's do that. I'm not ready to book, but I do want to understand the service.

- contemplate · v. · 认真考虑；义项 sense-staged-ox-48d8797ffa4aaadc-p10-b-28；来源 ox-48d8797ffa4aaadc；证据 p10-b-28-s01；词形 contemplate。
- counselor · n. · 咨询师；义项 sense-staged-ox-da6240d59b20fba1-p10-b-28；来源 ox-da6240d59b20fba1；证据 p10-b-28-s02；词形 counselor。
- conquer · v. · 克服；义项 sense-staged-ox-b5a736287e63d039-p10-b-28；来源 ox-b5a736287e63d039；证据 p10-b-28-s03；词形 conquer。
- counseling · n. · 心理咨询服务；义项 sense-staged-ox-33e4ecbea40f5ccf-p10-b-28；来源 ox-33e4ecbea40f5ccf；证据 p10-b-28-s04；词形 counseling。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The campus scene explores presentation anxiety without diagnosing or prescribing. s05 requests reading the first-conversation page together, s06 agrees, and s07 explicitly selects that step while declining to book now. The title, label and goal accurately describe a completed choice to learn more, not contact or treatment begun; outcome evidence supports the distinction.

## 约定先测试打印设置再判断故障 · p10-b-30

主题：消费与服务；情景：约定先测试打印设置再判断故障（printer-settings-fault-isolation）。

沟通目标：说明打印机反复恢复彩色设置，并约定核对默认配置、测试后回报结果，若问题仍出现则进一步调查

- p10-b-30-s02 · Support agent: Is colour still the default in your saved printer profile?
- p10-b-30-s03 · Anika: I don't know. I only change the setting in the print window.
- p10-b-30-s04 · Support agent: That may explain it, but it could also be a software defect. Let us check the saved configuration before deciding.
- p10-b-30-s05 · Anika: I can open the profile after this call. What result do you need?
- p10-b-30-s06 · Support agent: Set black and white as the saved choice, print one test page, then reopen the print window. Tell us whether the choice has stayed the same.
- p10-b-30-s07 · Anika: I'll try that with a blank test document and send you the result. If it reverts again, please investigate further.
- p10-b-30-s08 · Support agent: We will. I have added those test steps to your support ticket.

Full changed-file rereview accepted. s01-s04 establish repeated colour-setting resets, an unknown saved default and only a possible software fault. s06 proposes saving black-and-white, printing and reopening the window; s07 accepts testing and reporting, and s08 accepts further investigation only if the choice reverts. The revised goal and clause-level evidence now preserve this condition and no longer invent a support escalation. Title/label and context match the technical-support task; only the recording of test steps is completed, not diagnosis, testing or repair.

## 约定更正车站历史作业中的日期冲突表述 · p10-b-31

主题：学习与技能；情景：约定更正车站历史作业中的日期冲突表述（student-source-conflict-revision）。

沟通目标：核对两份材料分别记录的事件，并约定把首次客运与开幕仪式分开注明

背景：虚构地方史课堂：学生比较给定日记与报纸节录中的车站日期。

人物：Teacher、Elliot。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-31-s01 · Elliot: There is a contradiction here. The diary says the station opened in 1924, but the newspaper gives 1925.
- p10-b-31-s02 · Teacher: Look at what each writer was describing. The diary records a first passenger journey; the newspaper reports the official opening ceremony.
- p10-b-31-s03 · Elliot: So I cannot contend that the diary is wrong simply because the dates differ.
- p10-b-31-s04 · Teacher: Exactly. What looks like contrary evidence may concern a different event.
- p10-b-31-s05 · Elliot: My paragraph calls the diary unreliable. That needs a correction.
- p10-b-31-s06 · Teacher: How will you rewrite it?
- p10-b-31-s07 · Elliot: I'll give separate dates for the first passenger service and the official ceremony, with a reference for each.

- contradiction · n. · 矛盾；不一致；义项 sense-staged-ox-d87259528539da36-p10-b-31；来源 ox-d87259528539da36；证据 p10-b-31-s01；词形 contradiction。

- contrary · adj. · 相反的；义项 sense-staged-ox-7e9fcf12213a1555-p10-b-31；来源 ox-7e9fcf12213a1555；证据 p10-b-31-s04；词形 contrary。
- correction · n. · 更正；义项 sense-staged-ox-0f75ac2c334efd18-p10-b-31；来源 ox-0f75ac2c334efd18；证据 p10-b-31-s05；词形 correction。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional source-comparison task resolves an apparent date conflict by separating first passenger service from the later official ceremony. s03/s05 reject calling the diary unreliable solely from differing dates; s07 promises separate referenced entries. Title/label and goal correctly describe a revision agreement, not a changed document; source descriptions remain consistent after clarification.

- ox-d5ce587775a468c1：accepted；s03 contend that the diary is wrong uses the verb plus that-clause for asserting a position, explicitly denied as insufficiently supported.（p10-b-31-s03）。

## 约定整理驻外记者的采访往来邮件 · p10-b-32

主题：工作与协作；情景：约定整理驻外记者的采访往来邮件（foreign-correspondent-contact-record）。

沟通目标：明确需要查找的采访确认邮件，并约定按对象和日期整理供记者出发前核对

背景：新闻编辑室的同事整理一位驻外记者已有的工作邮件副本，不实际发送消息。

人物：Editor、Salma。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-32-s01 · Salma: Our foreign correspondent leaves on Monday. Has the interview with the port manager been confirmed?

- p10-b-37-s03 · Dara: But the evidence section sounds very serious.

- p10-b-37-s06 · Tutor: Yes. Keep that distinction clear and do not predict the result.

## 改用站立轻步的舞台入场方式 · p10-b-40

主题：文娱与运动；情景：改用站立轻步的舞台入场方式（stage-stealth-entrance-rehearsal）。

沟通目标：澄清角色偷偷入场的表演目的，并因服装活动限制决定不采用爬行

背景：虚构剧场排练：演员与导演讨论一位躲避舞台守卫的角色如何进场。

人物：Director、Milo。

起草：author-phase10-b；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-b-40-s01 · Milo: Do you want me to crawl under the bench when the guard turns away?
- p10-b-40-s02 · Director: No, just creep across the stage behind him. The audience should see you trying not to make a sound.
- p10-b-40-s03 · Milo: This long coat catches when I kneel. Staying on my feet would be much easier.
- p10-b-40-s04 · Director: Then keep upright and take small, quiet steps. We are showing caution, not an escape through a tunnel.
- p10-b-40-s05 · Milo: All right. I'll enter after the guard faces the window and stay behind him.
- p10-b-40-s06 · Director: Yes. Let's rehearse that standing entrance from his turn toward the window.

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-b；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional rehearsal establishes a guard, stage entrance and stealth purpose. s02 rejects crawling under the bench, s03 identifies the coat catching when kneeling, and s04-s06 settle upright quiet steps after the guard faces the window. Title/label and goal accurately describe a completed movement-plan decision with costume constraints, not a finished rehearsal; outcome evidence keeps that distinction.

- p10-c-01-s04 · Nurse Bell: No. The doctor needs to review the full report and talk to you. I can arrange a call for this afternoon.

- p10-c-01-s06 · Nurse Bell: Certainly. I have booked the call and added that question.

The six turns distinguish a possible deficiency from a diagnosis, then explicitly book the afternoon doctor call in s06. Title, label and both goal clauses correctly describe clarification and a booked discussion, not completed diagnosis or treatment.

- p10-c-07-s04 · Malcolm: She agreed to describe the incident, not to have her identity published. We need more discretion here.
- p10-c-07-s05 · Inez: Would removing her name be enough?
- p10-c-07-s06 · Malcolm: Not if her role identifies her. That would still be an unintended disclosure.
- p10-c-07-s07 · Inez: I will remove the name and the specific job title, keep the manager's response, and mark the disputed details as unverified.
- p10-c-07-s08 · Malcolm: Good. Send me that version before publication.

The dialogue separates the manager’s denial from verification, explains why a job title can identify the complainant, and agrees to remove name/title while retaining the response and uncertainty. Title, label and goal preserve the pre-publication and future-edit status; no leak or revision is claimed completed.

- p10-c-08-s03 · Sana: What about the actors? The file says they performed the banned play.

- p10-c-08-s05 · Sana: Then we need two separate descriptions: public criticism in the newspaper, and a prohibited performance at the theatre.

The students compare a fictional editorial and theatre record rather than make claims about a real regime. s02–s04 distinguish denunciation from violating a ban; s05–s06 agree separate descriptions/citations. Title, label and goal correctly describe a planned caption correction with no unsupported overthrow claim.

## 选定缓坡下山路线并约好同行 · p10-c-14

- p10-c-14-s01 · Leah: Do we descend by the steps or take the longer path?

## 确定漏水传感器试点的比较指标 · p10-c-19

- p10-c-19-s03 · Jae: Can we devise a test using the training tanks and the same small leaks for both sensors?
- p10-c-19-s04 · Morgan: Yes. To measure effectiveness, record missed leaks and false alarms as well as successful alerts.
- p10-c-19-s05 · Jae: And efficiency would include how much staff time the checks take?
- p10-c-19-s06 · Morgan: Exactly. A sensor can detect leaks well and still take too much work to maintain.
- p10-c-19-s07 · Jae: Then I will prepare a sheet with those three results and staff time, and we will test both sensors under the same conditions.
- p10-c-19-s08 · Morgan: Agreed. Let me check the sheet before the trial starts.

Title, label and goal describe these agreed comparison measures, not an established performance advantage or completed trial.

- p10-c-22-s06 · Beth: The venue has approved the indoor waiting area. The manager can open it when the outside queue reaches the marked point.

- p10-c-22-s08 · Beth: Good. I will keep the entrance clear and direct people once the manager opens the waiting area.

Title, label and goal retain contingency/arrangement status, not a current crowd emergency or already-open waiting area.

## 约好陪朋友整理洪灾援助文件 · p10-c-23

主题：家庭与人际；情景：洪灾后约定陪朋友整理救援文件（flood-friend-practical-support）。

沟通目标：倾听洪灾后的难过与压力，并约定次日上午一起整理援助文件

背景：洪灾后暂住安全住处的朋友谈论损失和眼下需要的实际帮助。

人物：Eva、Samir。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-23-s01 · Eva: I never thought one night of flooding could devastate our whole street.
- p10-c-23-s02 · Samir: I am so sorry. What would be useful today?
- p10-c-23-s03 · Eva: People keep saying I was lucky. I know they mean well, but I cannot endure another cheerful speech.
- p10-c-23-s04 · Samir: I do not want to diminish what you lost. You do not have to make it sound smaller for me.
- p10-c-23-s05 · Eva: The distress comes in waves. Then I look at the relief forms and cannot face them.
- p10-c-23-s06 · Samir: Would it ease the pressure if I sat with you while you sorted the papers? I would not fill anything in without you.
- p10-c-23-s07 · Eva: Yes. Could you come tomorrow at ten? I want company, not someone to take over.
- p10-c-23-s08 · Samir: I will be there at ten. We can put the papers in order and stop whenever you want.

- endure · v. · 忍受；义项 sense-staged-ox-866acfd5d86ed1f0-p10-c-23；来源 ox-866acfd5d86ed1f0；证据 p10-c-23-s03；词形 endure。
- diminish · v. · 贬低；淡化重要性；义项 sense-staged-ox-690ca63aefab669e-p10-c-23；来源 ox-690ca63aefab669e；证据 p10-c-23-s04；词形 diminish。

The flood, relief papers and friendship context support title/label/goal; no application or assistance is claimed completed.

- p10-c-25-s04 · Benji: Yes, it became the most common shape there. That does not mean it would be best in every environment.
- p10-c-25-s05 · Sofia: I will keep the change in frequency and remove the claim that it is universally superior.
- p10-c-25-s06 · Benji: Good. Add that the chart alone does not establish the cause of the change.

## 商定通过用户试用选择展示原型 · p10-c-27

主题：工作与协作；情景：在产品分歧中商定比较原型的方法（cofounder-prototype-choice-process）。

- p10-c-27-s01 · Nell: We have a dilemma: the simple prototype is easy to learn, but the other one shows more of what we can build.
- p10-c-27-s02 · Oscar: I prefer the second, though I do not want to dictate the choice just because I designed it.
- p10-c-27-s03 · Nell: I appreciate that. My ego is involved too; I have defended the simple one all week.
- p10-c-27-s04 · Oscar: Could we watch a few first-time users try both before we decide?
- p10-c-27-s05 · Nell: Yes. Give them the same task and do not explain the controls unless they ask.
- p10-c-27-s06 · Oscar: Then we can compare where they get stuck rather than which design we like more.
- p10-c-27-s07 · Nell: Agreed. I will arrange the sessions, and we will choose the demo version after reviewing them together.

## 确定公众历史工作坊的参与式结构 · p10-c-29

主题：学习与技能；情景：设计鼓励参与的公众历史工作坊（public-history-workshop-structure）。

沟通目标：明确工作坊讨论范围，并约定以口述故事、双人交流和开放提问鼓励参与

背景：成人教育负责人和受邀教育工作者筹备社区历史工作坊。

人物：June、Dr Mensah。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-29-s01 · June: As an educator, how would you make a local-history workshop feel less like a lecture?
- p10-c-29-s02 · Dr Mensah: Start with an engaging oral account, then ask people what they noticed before offering an interpretation.
- p10-c-29-s03 · June: We want the session to enrich public discourse, not just test who knows the most dates.
- p10-c-29-s04 · Dr Mensah: Then give people time to talk in pairs. Engagement is easier when every contribution does not have to be made to a whole room.
- p10-c-29-s05 · June: Should the discussion encompass the entire town history?
- p10-c-29-s06 · Dr Mensah: Not in one evening. Let us focus on how one street changed, using two residents' accounts and a map.
- p10-c-29-s07 · June: That gives us a clear structure: a short story, paired discussion, and open questions about that street.
- p10-c-29-s08 · Dr Mensah: Yes. I will choose the accounts, and you can prepare the paired-discussion prompt.

- p10-c-31-s03 · Amal: Does parliament also enforce every rule at the polling stations?
- p10-c-31-s04 · Finn: Not in this example. The independent election office handles enforcement, while the courts hear challenges.
- p10-c-31-s05 · Amal: Where do ordinary voters fit? We have drawn only institutions.
- p10-c-31-s06 · Finn: The notes give voters a public consultation stage before the law is passed. Let us add that as a separate route into the process.
- p10-c-31-s07 · Amal: Agreed. I will draw three institutional roles and a separate arrow for voters' comments, rather than putting every task under parliament.

## 确定学生奖项推荐的具体事例 · p10-c-33

主题：工作与协作；情景：用具体证据确定学生奖项推荐重点（student-award-reference-evidence）。

背景：美国学校的两名教师讨论一封尚未撰写的学生奖项推荐信。

人物：Ms Brooks、Mr Chen。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-33-s01 · Ms Brooks: The science fair results are encouraging. Should we nominate Lila for the student award?
- p10-c-33-s02 · Mr Chen: I think so, but the project was a collective endeavor. We should not give her credit for everyone's work.
- p10-c-33-s03 · Ms Brooks: What evidence of individual excellence can we point to?
- p10-c-33-s04 · Mr Chen: Her lab notes were exceptional. Other teams could repeat the experiment from her records without asking for missing steps.
- p10-c-33-s05 · Ms Brooks: She also helped two younger students set up their own records. I saw that during the practice session.
- p10-c-33-s06 · Mr Chen: Then those are the examples we should use, rather than just saying she won a prize.
- p10-c-33-s07 · Ms Brooks: Agreed. I will draft the nomination around her clear records and the help I observed, and name the project as team work.

- exceptional · adj. · 非同寻常地出色的；义项 sense-staged-ox-f2dc3959a608ba67-p10-c-33；来源 ox-f2dc3959a608ba67；证据 p10-c-33-s04；词形 exceptional。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

Final independent rereview actually read all seven current turns, all four target mappings, title, scenario label/goal, US-school context and the complete corrected outcome evidence. Mr Chen supplies the lab-record assessment in s04; Ms Brooks supplies her own observation of help to two younger students in s05 and consistently says “the help I observed” in s07. The corrected evidence now says 两名年龄较小的学生, with no unsupported school-grade inference. The title/goal accurately describe selecting concrete nomination examples, separating team achievement from personal contribution, and agreeing to draft rather than claiming a written/submitted nomination. Both the original speaker-attribution defect and the later evidence-specificity defect are resolved; no remaining substantive defect found.

## 决定拒绝附带独家背书条件的赞助 · p10-c-35

主题：工作与协作；情景：决定不接受带独家背书条件的赞助（sponsor-endorsement-boundary）。

沟通目标：核对赞助要求与活动独立性冲突，并决定拒绝当前条件

背景：社区修理工作坊组织者讨论一家工具商提供的赞助草案。

人物：Marcel、Faye。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-35-s01 · Marcel: The sponsor wants us to endorse its tools during every repair session.
- p10-c-35-s02 · Faye: I thought the offer only asked for a logo on the programme.
- p10-c-35-s03 · Marcel: The latest draft requires an exclusive endorsement. We could not recommend any competing tool.
- p10-c-35-s04 · Faye: That would stop our instructors from giving independent answers. We should not accept it.
- p10-c-35-s05 · Marcel: Could we ask for a simple logo acknowledgment instead?
- p10-c-35-s06 · Faye: We could, but they have already refused that twice. I would rather decline this offer than keep negotiating.
- p10-c-35-s07 · Marcel: I agree. I will write back declining the current terms and make clear that we are not promising any endorsement.

目标用法：

- endorse · v. · 公开支持；推荐；义项 sense-staged-ox-2095ef809285f1ca-p10-c-35；来源 ox-2095ef809285f1ca；证据 p10-c-35-s01；词形 endorse。
- exclusive · adj. · 排他的；独家的；义项 sense-staged-ox-ac854b8aee37b603-p10-c-35；来源 ox-ac854b8aee37b603；证据 p10-c-35-s03；词形 exclusive。
- endorsement · n. · 公开认可；背书；义项 sense-staged-ox-41a3b7325050b72f-p10-c-35；来源 ox-41a3b7325050b72f；证据 p10-c-35-s03；词形 endorsement。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The organizers discover the draft requires exclusive recommendations rather than just a logo, consider the already-refused alternative, and agree to decline the current terms. Title, label and goal distinguish their decision from the reply still to be written, with the tools sponsor and independence conflict explicitly established.

绑定文件 SHA-256：295f0ff70b7754e35aa70998cb13d3d2a26e661762442b575f0c871da772a979

- ox-2095ef809285f1ca：accepted；s01 “endorse its tools during every repair session” is a transitive verb for publicly recommend/support the products, not sign a financial instrument.（p10-c-35-s01）。
- ox-ac854b8aee37b603：accepted；s03 “an exclusive endorsement” is an attributive adjective meaning restricted to that sponsor, immediately clarified by the ban on recommending competitors.（p10-c-35-s03）。
- ox-41a3b7325050b72f：accepted；s03 “an exclusive endorsement” is a count noun for public product approval/recommendation, the specific contractual demand they reject.（p10-c-35-s03）。

## 决定先参加陶艺体验课再考虑正式报名 · p10-c-36

主题：学习与技能；情景：询问陶艺课程并选择先参加体验课（british-pottery-course-enquiry）。

沟通目标：询问陶艺课程的入门要求，并约好周六参加体验课

背景：英国社区陶艺工作室的前台与首次来访者交谈。

人物：Imogen、Stan。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-36-s01 · Imogen: I would like to enquire about your evening pottery course. Is it suitable for a complete beginner?
- p10-c-36-s02 · Stan: Yes, but if you are unsure, you can try the Saturday taster before committing to six weeks.
- p10-c-36-s03 · Imogen: Do I need to enrol on the full course to attend the taster?
- p10-c-36-s04 · Stan: No. The taster has its own booking list, and tools are provided.
- p10-c-36-s05 · Imogen: Then could you put me down for the taster this Saturday?
- p10-c-36-s06 · Stan: Certainly. I have reserved a place under Imogen. It starts at eleven.
- p10-c-36-s07 · Imogen: Thank you. I will come at eleven and decide about the full course afterwards.

目标用法：

- enquire · v. · 询问；义项 sense-staged-ox-0d9291177bd4284c-p10-c-36；来源 ox-0d9291177bd4284c；证据 p10-c-36-s01；词形 enquire。
- enrol · v. · 报名；注册（英式拼写）；义项 sense-staged-ox-5dc6bc48674f7e7d-p10-c-36；来源 ox-5dc6bc48674f7e7d；证据 p10-c-36-s03；词形 enrol。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The pottery studio confirms beginner suitability, distinguishes the Saturday taster from full-course enrolment, and explicitly reserves Imogen a place at eleven. Title, label and goal accurately claim a taster booking and deferred six-week decision, not full-course registration or attendance already completed.

绑定文件 SHA-256：9b180ac74aec6138bf01f79464401ad9cc192f28d6f85b4ea14acbaacda76eac

- ox-0d9291177bd4284c：accepted；s01 “enquire about your evening pottery course” uses the verb with about for requesting information, naturally followed by a suitability question in British usage.（p10-c-36-s01）。
- ox-5dc6bc48674f7e7d：accepted；s03 “enrol on the full course” is the British-spelled intransitive verb with on-course complement; the question distinguishes course registration from taster booking.（p10-c-36-s03）。

## 核对写作课程资格并完成报名 · p10-c-37

主题：学习与技能；情景：核对写作课程资格并完成报名（american-writing-course-eligibility）。

沟通目标：根据课程说明确认资格，提交报名表并收到确认编号

背景：美国社区学院的写作课程报名台上，申请者核对课程说明并操作报名网页。

人物：Devon、Clerk Ames。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-37-s01 · Devon: I want to enroll in the evening writing course, but I have never studied literature.
- p10-c-37-s02 · Clerk Ames: That is fine. The course page says anyone eighteen or over is eligible; there is no prior-study requirement.
- p10-c-37-s03 · Devon: I am twenty-four. I have completed the form and read the course terms. Is this the final submit button?
- p10-c-37-s04 · Clerk Ames: Yes. Check that you selected the evening group before you submit.
- p10-c-37-s05 · Devon: The evening group is selected. I have submitted it now, and the screen shows confirmation W-218.
- p10-c-37-s06 · Clerk Ames: That means your registration was received. The confirmation lists your first session next Tuesday at six.
- p10-c-37-s07 · Devon: Great. I have saved it, and I will be here on Tuesday.

目标用法：

- enroll · v. · 报名；注册（美式拼写）；义项 sense-staged-ox-fe287064fd18ef36-p10-c-37；来源 ox-fe287064fd18ef36；证据 p10-c-37-s01；词形 enroll。
- eligible · adj. · 符合资格的；义项 sense-staged-ox-8f57a2e3db249f20-p10-c-37；来源 ox-8f57a2e3db249f20；证据 p10-c-37-s02；词形 eligible。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The page’s age and prior-study requirements are checked against the adult applicant. s05 explicitly reports submission and confirmation W-218; s06 explains receipt and supplies the first session, and s07 saves it. Title, label and goal therefore correctly claim completed registration/confirmation, not mere form preparation.

绑定文件 SHA-256：9fe7b405bdc73c4618b6c8661bdb621254dc83f7dc3dc48dea56980f42777cf1

- ox-fe287064fd18ef36：accepted；s01 “enroll in the evening writing course” uses the American-spelled intransitive verb with in-course complement, consistent with the US college context.（p10-c-37-s01）。
- ox-8f57a2e3db249f20：accepted；s02 “anyone eighteen or over is eligible” is a predicative adjective for meeting the stated entry condition; s03 confirms this applicant is twenty-four.（p10-c-37-s02）。

## 因设备维修改约服务时间 · p10-c-38

主题：消费与服务；情景：因维修中断调整服务预约（repair-appointment-disruption-choice）。

沟通目标：核对设备维修造成的服务中断，并把自行车保养预约改到次日

背景：自行车维修店临时维修车间设备，顾客来电核对原预约。

人物：Penny、Ash。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-38-s01 · Penny: Will the equipment repairs disrupt my bicycle service this afternoon?
- p10-c-38-s02 · Ash: I am afraid so. The workshop lift is out of use, and the disruption will last until closing.
- p10-c-38-s03 · Penny: I need the bike tomorrow evening. Could you do the service tomorrow morning instead?
- p10-c-38-s04 · Ash: We have a nine o'clock slot, with collection by three if the service reveals no additional work.
- p10-c-38-s05 · Penny: Please move my booking to nine. If anything else needs doing, call me before you start it.
- p10-c-38-s06 · Ash: Done. Your booking is now tomorrow at nine, and I have noted that we need your approval for any extra work.
- p10-c-38-s07 · Penny: Thanks. I will bring it in at nine.

目标用法：

- disrupt · v. · 扰乱；使中断；义项 sense-staged-ox-9a238a67c42f9f21-p10-c-38；来源 ox-9a238a67c42f9f21；证据 p10-c-38-s01；词形 disrupt。
- disruption · n. · 中断；扰乱；义项 sense-staged-ox-1c5427fe5eda82af-p10-c-38；来源 ox-1c5427fe5eda82af；证据 p10-c-38-s02；词形 disruption。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The bicycle shop’s lift repair causes afternoon disruption; the customer requests the next morning and Ash explicitly completes the nine-o’clock booking change in s06. Title, label and goal accurately describe completed rescheduling, while collection by three remains conditional and extra work requires approval.

绑定文件 SHA-256：0f313af998638f7d6dc53df7193e0b32ca6c3dba93e9b87bdb5267da5459da02

- ox-1c5427fe5eda82af：accepted；s02 “the disruption will last until closing” is an uncountable event/state noun for the service interruption, with a defined duration.（p10-c-38-s02）。

<a id="dialogue-p10-c-39"></a>

## 约定重写虚构疫情图表的标题 · p10-c-39

主题：研究与论证；情景：限定虚构疫情报道标题的证据范围（fictional-epidemic-headline-check）。

沟通目标：区分新增报告数与现存病例总数，并约定删去夸大疫情已经结束的标题

背景：媒体分析课堂中，两名学生检查虚构疫情案例的一张每周报告图表。

人物：Luca、Nia。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-39-s01 · Luca: The case study describes an epidemic, but the last point on this chart is much lower. Can our headline say it is over?
- p10-c-39-s02 · Nia: No. The chart shows new reports each week, not the total number of people still ill.
- p10-c-39-s03 · Luca: I do not want to exaggerate the improvement, but we should acknowledge the drop.
- p10-c-39-s04 · Nia: Of course. Saying there were fewer new reports in the final week would not distort what the chart actually shows.
- p10-c-39-s05 · Luca: Shall we use that as the headline and add that the chart cannot tell us whether transmission has stopped?
- p10-c-39-s06 · Nia: Yes. Replace "Epidemic over" with the narrower statement, and keep the dates from the chart.

目标用法：

- epidemic · n. · 流行病；疫情；义项 sense-staged-ox-33fc950618532314-p10-c-39；来源 ox-33fc950618532314；证据 p10-c-39-s01；词形 epidemic。
- exaggerate · v. · 夸大；义项 sense-staged-ox-bdfa1492d6f78077-p10-c-39；来源 ox-bdfa1492d6f78077；证据 p10-c-39-s03；词形 exaggerate。
- distort · v. · 歪曲；使失真；义项 sense-staged-ox-5ccd44dd335133b1-p10-c-39；来源 ox-5ccd44dd335133b1；证据 p10-c-39-s04；词形 distort。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The fictional classroom chart concerns weekly new reports, not current total illness or proof transmission stopped. Both speakers agree to replace “Epidemic over” with a narrower final-week decline statement and keep dates. Chinese title, label and goal accurately describe this agreed correction without asserting a real outbreak ended.

绑定文件 SHA-256：33584478e5416057c769f0e44e1778a41530eddc7860481bb03c2ec4682138ec

- ox-33fc950618532314：accepted；s01 “describes an epidemic” is a count noun for a widespread disease outbreak in the case study; it is not an adjective or a live-news claim.（p10-c-39-s01）。
- ox-bdfa1492d6f78077：accepted；s03 “exaggerate the improvement” is a transitive verb for overstating how much conditions improved, the headline risk under discussion.（p10-c-39-s03）。
- ox-5ccd44dd335133b1：accepted；s04 “distort what the chart actually shows” is a transitive verb with a content-clause object for misrepresenting evidence, correctly negated for the narrower statement.（p10-c-39-s04）。

## 把合作咖啡馆理念落实为开业接待安排 · p10-c-40

主题：社区与公共空间；情景：把合作咖啡馆理念落实为开业接待安排（cooperative-cafe-opening-values）。

沟通目标：讨论合作咖啡馆希望体现的平等交流，并约定设置共用桌和轮流迎宾

背景：一家由员工共同经营的咖啡馆开业前，两名成员安排迎宾。

人物：Lottie、Mo。

起草：author-phase10-c；状态：审阅接受。

- p10-c-40-s01 · Lottie: People keep asking whether this will be a formal establishment with reserved tables and a host at the door.
- p10-c-40-s02 · Mo: I hope not. We want the cafe to embody the idea that neighbours can sit down and talk as equals.
- p10-c-40-s03 · Lottie: Then let us use the long shared table rather than setting every table for separate groups.
- p10-c-40-s04 · Mo: Agreed, but leave the small tables too. Not everyone will want company.
- p10-c-40-s05 · Lottie: Who should welcome people on opening day?
- p10-c-40-s06 · Mo: We can take turns instead of having one person act as the owner. That reflects how we run the place together.
- p10-c-40-s07 · Lottie: Good. I will set up the shared table and keep the smaller ones. You take the first welcome shift, and I will take the second.
- p10-c-40-s08 · Mo: That works. Let us put those two shifts on the rota.

目标用法：

- establishment · n. · 营业场所；机构；义项 sense-staged-ox-2302716b0fe29548-p10-c-40；来源 ox-2302716b0fe29548；证据 p10-c-40-s01；词形 establishment。
- embody · v. · 体现；使具体化；义项 sense-staged-ox-5be62b90e3ba134b-p10-c-40；来源 ox-5be62b90e3ba134b；证据 p10-c-40-s02；词形 embody。

审阅者：reviewer-phase10-c；整篇结论：accepted。

The worker-run cafe’s equal-conversation aim is translated into a shared table while retaining small tables, and two named welcome shifts are agreed for opening day. Shared operation is explicit in context and s06; title, label and goal describe a practical arrangement, not completed furniture setup or opening.

绑定文件 SHA-256：a6bf3c591d8afb2f91af73af7a7124b4f6a0785fa0f5ed9550e3ee92d115a8e5

- ox-2302716b0fe29548：accepted；s01 “a formal establishment with reserved tables” is a count noun for the business venue, not the process of establishing it or the political establishment.（p10-c-40-s01）。
- ox-5be62b90e3ba134b：accepted；s02 “cafe to embody the idea” is a transitive infinitive for make an abstract value concrete; shared seating and rotating welcome duties provide the practical expression.（p10-c-40-s02）。
